# Life of Abuna Demyanos (Il Gädl di Abunä Dəmyanos, santo eritreo)

**Author**: Anonymous  
**Series**: PO Tome 50

[p. 134] {#po.50.p134}

### THE GADL OF ABUNA DIMYANOS
ገድለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ። በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ዘገብረ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ በወልዱ፣ ምስለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ በሥምረት ፡ አሓዱ ፡ አሠርገዉ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ በድዱ። ወለምድርኒ ፡ አሠርገዋ፤ ፡ በጽጌ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ዘበ ፡ በዘመዱ ። ገብሩ ፡ ማዓምቅተ' ፡ ማያተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይትዔለዱ ። እምድኅረ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ጠፈረ ፡ በነፋስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዔምዱ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ አዝርእተ ፡ ወአትክልተ” ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ፈቀዱ ። ፀሓይ ፡ ወወርኅ ፡ ወከዋክብት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትባረዩ ፡ አዝማነ ፡ ዓለም ፡ እለ ፡ የአውዱ ። ገብሩ ፡ ዓሣተ ፡ ወአናብርተ ፡ ወአፅዋፈ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይፈቀዱ'። ገብሩ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ወአራዊተ ፡ ወኩሎ” ፡ ዘይትሐወስ ፡ ወየሐውር ፤ በከብዱ ። ወይቤሉ ፡ ንግበር ፡ ሰብአ ፡ በአርአያነ ፡ ወበአምሳሊነ ፡ ከመ ፡ እለ ፡ ፈጠርነ" ፡ ይግረሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰግዱ ፤ እምሥላሴሆሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይፈደፍዱ ። ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ወአስተዋደዱ ። አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በሥምረት ፡ አሓዱ። ዝኩ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ትጉሃነ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ስብሐቶ ፡ ዘያግህዱ ። ወእምግርማ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይርዕዱ ፡ አክሊላቲሆሙ ፡ ይወስዱ ።
Prologue 1 In the name of God who created the universe through his Son (Jn 1:3; Col 1:15-20; Heb 1:1-3). In full agreement with the Holy Spirit the Paraclete he adorned the sky with foundations And decorated the earth with pure buds of every kind He made the abysses where the waters of the seas gather. After having created the firmament which rests on the wind (En 18:2-3) they made the seeds and the plants according to their good pleasure. (They created) the sun, the moon, and the stars which rotate while the epochs of the world alternate; they created the fish, the cetaceans, and the birds so that they might be food for those who desire it. They made the animals, the beasts, every (creature) that moves and that crawls on its belly. And they said: "Let us make man in our image and likeness!" (Gn 1:26), so that these whom we have created may submit to Him when they prostrate, (they created him) from their Trinity without increasing. All this They made and harmonized. The Father and the Son and the Holy Spirit with a single will. It is this glory of his that the heavenly watchers (En 1:5 etc.; Rev 4:10) make explicit. Trembling at his magnificent power they lay down their crowns, while they prostrate before his throne (Rev 4:10), as John "Abugalamsis" son of Zebedee (Mk 3:17) has recounted. To the Trinity our God (be) glory and honor, greatness, power, and blessing, on earth and in the heavens, in the sea and in the abysses for the centuries of centuries. Rhymed panegyric in praise of Abuna Demyanos
ቅድመ ፡ መንበሩ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይሰግዱ ። በከመ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አቡቀለምስስ'' ፡ ለዘብዴዎስ ፡ ወልዱ ። ለሥላሴ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ስብሓት ፡ ወክብር ፡ ዕበይ ፡ ወሥልጣን ፡ ወበረከት ፡ በምድር ፡ ወሰማያት ፡ በባሕር ፡ ወበቀላያት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ንጽሕፍ ፡ እንከ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ስሙዓ ፡ ዜና ፡ ከመ ፡ ኤርምያስ ። ለሕገ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ቀናኢ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኤልያስ ። ረአዬ ፡ ኅቡአት ፤ ከመ ፡ ሕዝቅኤል ፡ ወዳንኤል ፤ ወኢሳይያስ ። ምሉአ ፡ ይዋሄ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሙሴ፤! ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወዘካርያስ ። መስተዓግስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየብ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ሐኪም ፡
2 We write therefore the history of the blessed man of God our father Damyanos, of famous history like (that of) Jeremiah. Zealous for the law of his God like Elijah. Seer of hidden things like Ezekiel, Daniel, and Isaiah, filled with meekness like Moses, David, and Zechariah.

ወመሐኪም" ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ። ጸዋሬ ፡ ተጽናስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጦቢት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ራጌስ ። ተቃሐዊ ፡ ለምግባረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሆሴዕ ፡ ወአሞጽ ፡ ወሚክያስ ። ተፃራሪ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጣዖተ ፡ አብዳን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢዩኤል ፡ ወአብድዩ ፡ ረድአ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ዘዘለፎሙ ፡ ለካህናተ ፡ ሐስዎ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ አካኣብ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ዘይፈቱ ፡ ወይጽሕቅ ፡ ለአድኅኖተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ዘሰበከ ፡ ምህላ" ፡ ወኦ ፡፤ እምባሕረ ፡ ተርሴስ ። መቅለደ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ከመ ፡ ናሆም ፡ ወእንባቆም" ፡ ወሰፎንያስ ። ዜናዌ ፤ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወኃሜት ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐጌ ፡ ወዘካርያስ ፡ ወምልክያስ ። ወልደ ፡ ትምህርቶሙ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ ወለጳ ውሎስ” ። ገባሬ ፡ ተአምር ፡ በረዲኤተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ፍጹመ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ከመ ፡ አቡነ' ፡ እንጦንዮስ ። ወትሩፈ ፡ ገድል ፡ ከመ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መቃርስ ።
Patient like the holy Job. Learned, most learned like King Solomon. Capable of enduring affliction like Tobit of the city of Rages (Tob 1:14). Zealous in performing the works of justice (Tit 2:14) like Hosea, Amos, and Micah. Opponent of the idol of the foolish like Joel and Obadiah disciple of Elijah (1 Kgs 18:3ff) who condemned the false priests at the time of King Ahab (Damyanos) who desires with zeal the salvation of men like Jonah who, leaving the sea of Tarshish (Jon 1:3-4), invited to conversion. Basin of prophecy like Nahum and Habakkuk and Zephaniah. Messenger of joy and gladness like Haggai, Zechariah, and Malachi. Son of the doctrine of Peter and of Paul, wonder-worker with the help of the Holy Spirit. Of perfect monasticism like [our father Anthony. Of a life overflowing (with grace and virtue) like our father Macarius
መምህረ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ከመ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮሓንስ ፡ ወከመ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ። ንጹሐ ፤ ተክህኖ ፡ በቀኒተ ፡ ኤፉድ ፡ ሐዲስ ። ንጹሐ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወነፍስ ። መያጤ ፡ ጊጉያን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘይሜኒ ፡ መርስ ። እንዘ ፡ ይተሉ ፡ በግዕዘ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወበግዕዘ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወንኡስ ፡ በከመ ፡ መሀሮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኮንክዎሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ አይሁ ዳዊ” ፡ ከመ ፡ አስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ወኮንክዎሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ከመ ፡ አስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ። ወኮንክ ዎሙ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢኮንኩ ፡ ኅጥአ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ አስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ። ወለድኩማን ፡ ኮንክዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ድኩም ፡ ከመ ፡ አስተ ጋብኦሙ ፡ ለድኩማን ። ወበግፅዕዘ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ተለውኩ ፡ ወገበርኩ ፡ ወከመ ፡ አስተጋብ ኦሙ ፡ መንገሌየ ። ወኩሉ ፡ዘእገብር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ከመ ፡ እኩኖሙ፣፤ ሱታፎሙ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ተለወ ፡ በግዕዘ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መርሐ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ በረከቱ ፡ ተሃሉ ፡ ምስሌነ” ፡ አሜን” ። ንነግረክሙ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሠርፀ ፡ ሥርዉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ። አንቀጸ ፡ ሙፃኢሁ ፡ እምአቡነ ፡ አብሳዲ ፡ ሰብለ ፡ ትምህርቱ ፡ ለኣቡነ ፡ ሙሴ ። ፀዋሬ፣
3 Master of the law like John and our father Pachomius. Of unblemished priesthood with the belt of a new ephod (Ex 28:6). Pure in flesh and in soul. He who leads the strayed back to the pleasant harbor following each in the most appropriate way, adapting himself to the conduct of the adult and the minor, as our father Paul taught him when he says: "I became a Jew to the Jews that I might win the Jews, to those who are under the law I became as one under the law, that I might win those under the law. I became as a sinner to the sinners, though I was not a sinner before the Lord, that I might win the sinners. To the weak, I became as a weak one that I might gather the weak. I followed the behavior of each and acted (thus) to draw them to me. And I do everything for the teaching of the Gospel that I might be a partaker of it" (1 Cor 9:20-23). Having heard this, our father Damyanos adapted himself to the behavior of all and became a guide to all, may his blessing be with us Amen! 4 We narrate to you the history of our father for he is the sprout of the root of our father Ewostatewos his origin is from our father Absadi


[p. 138] {#po.50.p138}
ትንቢቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ተንባሊ ፡ ወኢያብጣሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጳውሊ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ”፤ ፡ አሜን” ።
the ear of the teaching of our father Muse, depositary of the prophecy of our father Sam'on, irreproachable intercessor like our father Pawli. May his prayer and his blessing be with us Amen

### Supplication of the hagiographer

5 Grant me O Lord wisdom and sense so that I may narrate the history of this spiritual father. Grant me O Lord science and discernment, together with the fear of God, so that I may narrate the history of this righteous master of the law. Grant me O Lord, the gift of healing and the spirit of wisdom so that I may narrate the history of this father, of the fluent speech. Grant me O Lord wisdom (Is 11:2), and put on my chest science, as you put (it) on the chest of the prophet Ezra (4 Esd 14:40), until he had the ninety-four books written, without drinking water and without touching food until the end of the forty days (4 Esd 14:44ff). In the same way, gather wisdom on my chest so that I may narrate the history of this meek lamb (Jer 11:19) who was chosen as guide of the sheep of the flock. Grant me Lord, wisdom and eloquent lips so that I may speak of the tender nature of this good shepherd (Jn 10:11) who pastures the sheep of the flock with the three rods of the law, leading them to the beautiful and fertile meadows of the Gospel. And when he leads them to the pastures he goes before them (Jn 10:4) on the path. All follow him, and if they follow him he will love them and will water them at a single well and at a single spring which is our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, to Him be glory together with his clement Father and the Holy Spirit, for the centuries of centuries, Amen.

The birth and monastic vocation of Abuna Demyanos 6 There was a man in the country of Bur and his name was Bagada Agzi'a, he Bagada Agzi'a belonged to the great ones of the country and to a very honored kinship. (Bagada Agzi'a) married a woman who was called Susanna and the Lord opened her womb and she bore for him sons and daughters, males and females. They gave them a good education, imparted to them the law of God and the worship that was due to Him. And one of them by the name of Mahari Krastos, grew up and having reached the age of marriage, they made him marry according to the law of marriage. Some days later, the Holy Spirit, according to (his) habit, made him reflect. Indeed (the Holy Spirit) dwells in the saints and makes them think of the salvation of their soul and this Sanbat Mahari meditated and remembered the word of our father Paul who says: "Brothers, it is right that it should be only so, for the condition of this world is transitory; and those

who are married (should be) as if they were not married. Those who are well as if they were not well, those who rejoice as if they were not rejoicing. And those who eat as if they were not eating. Those who have drunk as if they had not drunk. For the sojourn in this world is transitory and I desire that you live without sadness. And he who is not married thinks of the Lord and of how to please God, while the married one thinks of the condition of this world" (1 Cor 7:29-32). Again he remembered the word of our Lord Jesus Christ who in the Gospel says: "Whoever wants to join me, let him hate his soul and abandon it, and he who abandons his soul for me will find it" (Mk 8:34, 35). And he went out when he was meditating on this and suddenly renounced worldly values to follow the Father of Jesus Christ and with the help of God he strove to execute his commandments. He abandoned his mother and leaned on the mother of Jesus Christ, our Lady Mary, Virgin in two ways

### Vision of the heavenly Jerusalem
ኃደገ ፡ አኃወ ፡ ወአኃተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ እኅዋሆሙ ፡ ለማኅበረ ፡ መላእክት ፤ ለማኅበረ” ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ወሰማዕት ። ኃደገ ፤ መርዓተ ፡ ሐዳሰ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርከባ ፣ ለመርዓተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለዋ ፡ ትወርድ ፡ ዲበ ምድር ፡ ለተድላ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወኅሩያን ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ወሰማዕታት ፡ ደናግል ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ወሕፃናት ፡ እለ ፥ ንጹሓን ፡ እምርስሐተ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ እለ ሐፀቡ ፡ አልባሲሆሙ ፡ በደመ ፡ በግዑ ። በከመ ፡ ነገረ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ራእዩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡፤ አቡቀለምሲስ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ትዕግሥቶሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ጸማ ሆሙ ፡፤ ወግብሮሙ ። ወበእንተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ርስተ ፡ ተድላሆሙ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ ነዓ?" ፡ አርኢ ካሃ ፡ ለመርዓተ ፡ በግዑ ። ይቤ ፤ ወወሰደኒ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወነ ዊኅ ። ወአርአየኒሃ ፡ ለሀገር ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እንተ ፡ ትወርድ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወስብሐተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሃ ። ወከዋክብቲሃኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፅንሦ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወጽዕዱት ፡ ከመ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ ወዓቢይ ፡ አረፍታ ፡ ወነዋኅ ፡ ጥቅማ፣
7 He abandoned brothers and sisters to become a brother of the congregation of the angels and of the congregation of the saints and of the martyrs. He left the new bride to obtain the bride of the king, the holy and heavenly Zion which must descend upon the earth (Rev 21:9-10) for the joy of the saints and of the chosen prophets and apostles, of the saints and of the martyrs, of the virgins and of the monks, of the children free from the stain of sin, who have washed their garments in the blood of the Lamb (Rev 7:14), as our father John "Abugalamsis" said in his vision about them and about their patience, about their toil and about their work (Rev 7:14). And about Zion, the inheritance of their joy he said to me: "Come so that I may show you the bride of the lamb!" So he said and the Spirit led me to a great and high mountain and made me see the holy city, Jerusalem, which was descending from heaven from God and upon it was the glory of God, its stars were like precious gems and shining like pearls, its walls were great and high its ramparts and it had twelve gates.
ወባቲ ፡፲፣ወ፪፡፤ አናቅጽ ። ወውስተ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ፡ አናቅጽ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ፤ መላእክት ። ወጽሑፍ ፡ አስማተ ፡፲ወ፪፣፤ ሕዝበ” ፡ እስራኤል ። ውስተ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ጽባሐዊያን ፤ ጅወውስተ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ዓረባዊ ያን ፡ ፻ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ሰሜናዊያን” ፡ ፻ ወውስተ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ደቡባዊያን ፡፻ ፡ ወ፲ወ፪ ፤ መሰረታቲሃ ፡ ለአረፍተ ፡ ሀገራ ፡ ወጽሑፍ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ አስማተ ፡፲ወ፪፣ ሐዋርያተ ፡ በግዑ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ዘይትናገር ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ይጸውር ፡ ሕለተ፤ ፡ አምጣነ ፡ በዘቦቱ ፡ ይሜጥና ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወለአናቅጺሃ ፡ ወለአረፋቲሃ ። ወርብዕት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአምጣነ ፡ ኑኃ ፡ ግድማ ፡ ወመጠና ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ሕለት” ።
8 "And upon the twelve gates there were twelve angels and there were the writings of the names of the twelve tribes of the people of Israel: on the eastern gate three (names), on the western gate three (names), on the northern gate three (names), on the southern gate three (names). The walls of the city had twelve foundations. In them was written the name of the twelve apostles of the Lamb. He who spoke with me, carried [the rod of the measure to measure that city, its gates and its walls. That city is square as to its height and width. And the bulk of that city was of such proportions. Its measures

ወኮነ ፡ አምጣኒሃ ፡ ፲ ወ ፪፻ ፡ ኑሐ ፡ ወግድማ ፡ ወቆማኒ ፡ ዕሩይ ። ወእምዝ ፡ መጠነ አረፋቲሃ ፡፻ ወ ዓ ወ፪ ፡ በእመተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ፳8 በእመተ ፡ መልአክ ። ወውስተ፣ አረፍተ ፡ ሀገራ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ በፅንቄ ፡ ኢያስኢስ ፡ ወትመስል ፡ ሀገራ ፡ ከመ ፣ ዘበወርቅ ፡ ንጹሕ ፤ ግብረታ ። ወመሠረተ ፡ አረፍተ ፡ ሀገራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማኅው ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ሥርግው ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዕንቅ ፡ ክቡር ። ቀዳሚት ፡ መሠረት ፡ ዘኢያስሏስ ፡ ወካልእት ፡ በሰንፔር ። ወሣልስት ፡፤ በኬልቄ ዶን ። ወራብዕት ፡ በመረግድ ። ወኃምስት ፡ በስርዶንክስ ። ወሳድስት ፡ በሰርድዮን ። ወሳብዕት ፡ በክርሶሊቶቤ” ። ወሳምንት ፡ በቢረሌ ። ወታስዕት ፣ በወራውሬ ። ወዐስ ርት ፡ በክርስጳራስስ ። ወእንተ ፡ ዐስሩ ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ በያክንት ። ወዐስሩ ፡ ወክልኤ ፡፤ በአማቲስጢኖስ” ፡ ወዴዴያቲሃኒ ፡ በዓሥሩ ፡ ወክልኤ ፡ ባሕርይ ። ለለ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዴዴ አሓቲ ፡ ባሕርይ ። ወጽጐ ፡ሀገራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ዘያጸድል ፡ እንተ ፡ ውስጡ፣፤ ከመ ፡ ማኅው ። ወኢርኢኩ ፡ ጽርኀ" ፡ ዘከማሃ ፣ እስመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኩሎ ፡ ይመልክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ መቅደሱ ፡ ወበግዑ ። ወሀገራሰ ፡ ኢትፈቅድ ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ ወወርኀጎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብርሁ ፡ ላቲ ። እስመ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ላቲ ፤ ወማኅቶተ ፡ በግዑ ። ወየሐውሩ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ በብርሃነ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ወነገሥተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡ ክብሮሙ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኢይትዓፀዉ ፡ አናቅጺሃ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡
were of one thousand two hundred: its height, its width, and its stature were equal. He then measured its walls: (the measure is) of one hundred and forty-four human cubits which is equivalent to a cubit of an angel. Inside the walls of that city, there is a city with jasper pearls and it seems made of pure gold. And the foundation of the walls of the city is of white crystal, studded with every (kind of) precious gem. 9 "The first foundation is of jasper, the second of sapphire, the third of chalcedony, the fourth of emerald, the fifth of sardonyx, the sixth of carnelian, the seventh of chrysolite, the eighth of beryl, the ninth of marble, the tenth of chrysoprase, the eleventh of hyacinth, the twelfth of amethyst. And its gates (were made of) twelve pearls: each gate (was made) from one pearl. The square of the city was like pure gold which made what was inside shine like crystal. And I have not seen a hall similar to it, for there is in it God who dominates everything in his holy temple, and his Lamb. And the city does not need the sun and the moon, because they shine in it. It is in fact the light of the Lord and the lamp of the Lamb that illuminates it and the peoples will walk in its light and the kings of the earth will bring into it their glory and its gates will not close neither by day nor by night, for it will not have night.
እስመ ፡ አልባቲ ፡ ሌሊተ ። ወያመጽኡ ፡ ክብሮሙ ፡ ወስብሐቲሆሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወኢይበውእ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘርኩስ ፡ ወዘይገብር ፡ ገመነ ፡ ወሐሰተ ፡ ኢይጽሐፍ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘበግዑ ። ወአርአየኒ ፡ ፈለገ” ፡ ዘማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ በረድ ። ወይወፅእ ፡ እምነ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወስልጣነ ፡ በግዑ ፡ ወይውኅዝ ፡ ውስተ” ፡ ማእከለ ፡ መርኅባ ፡ እምለፌኒ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ፈለግ ፣ ወእምከሀከኒ” ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፡፤ ወይገብር ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ለለ ፤ አሐዱ ፡ ወርኅ ። ወይሁብ ፡ ፍሬሁ ፡ ወቄ ጽለ ፡ ዕፁ ፡ ለሕይወተ ፡ አሕዛብ ። ወአልቦ ፡ እንከ ፡ ርኩስ፤ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ” ። ወሀ ለዉ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ መንበረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበግዑ ፡ ወአግብርቲሁኒ ፡ ያመልክዎ ፡ ወይሬእይዎ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ወስሞ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍጽሞሙ ። ወአልቦ ፡ እንከ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወኢ ይፈቅዱ ፡ እንከ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወይነግሥ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
10 "And the peoples will bring into it their honor and their glory. Nothing impure will enter into it and he who commits abomination and falsehood will not be registered in the book of life of the Lamb. And he showed me the river of the water of life, white as snow, and it flowed from the throne of God and from the power of the Lamb. And it flowed in the middle of the square and on one side and on the other of the river was the tree of life which produces twelve fruits for each month. It produces its fruits and the leaves of the tree (are) to heal the peoples. There is therefore no impurity. And in it there are the throne of God and the Lamb and his servants adore him and see his face and his name is on their forehead. There is no longer night and they will no longer need the light of the sun because God will illuminate them and will reign for the centuries of centuries" (Rev 21:9-22:5). On the way to the monastery of Dabra Salam and the meeting with Abuna Muse
ወዘንተኬ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ተሰፊዎ ፡ መነነ ፣ መፍቅዳተ ፡ ዘበምድር ፡ ወፈለሰ ፡ ርቀ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ደብር ፡ እምአድባረ ፡ ሠራዊዌ ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ፣ ደብረ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሙሴ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዓቃቤ ፡ ነግድ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፳ወሬዛ ፤ ዘተለዎ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለዓቃቤ ፡ ነግድ ፡ አብጽሐነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፤ እስመ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ መፍቅድ ፡ ብነ ። ወሖረ ፡ ዓቃቤ ፡ ነግድ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወይቤ ፤ አውሎግሶን ፡ በከመ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፣ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ ወራዙት ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዴዴ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ወይብሉ ፡ ብነ ፡
11 (Abuna Damyanos) placed his trust in this wealth of grace, renounced the goods of the earth and emigrated to a distant country and reached a mountain among the mountains of Sarawe by the name of Dabra Salam the mountain of our father Muse. He presented himself to the guest-master of the monastery together with a young man who had followed him and they said to him: "Take us to the abbot because we have a great necessity." He went to the abbot and according to the custom of the monks said: "Awlogson!" He spoke to him about them, saying:


[p. 144] {#po.50.p144}
መፍቅድ”' ፡ ኅበ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወሠናያን ፡ ራእዮሙ ፣ ወሥእሱእች ፡ አውሥኦተ ፤ ቃሎሙ ፡ ወኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ መንፈሰዎ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወይመስሉ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ መኳንንት ። ወይቤ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ለዓቃቤ ፡ ነግድ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ኀቤየ ። ወሖረ ፡ ዓቃቤ ፡ ነግድ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ በፍሥሠሕ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ወበብሩህ ፡ገጽ ፡ ወበሠናይ ፡ አውሥኦተ”ዎ ፡ ቃል ፡ እስመ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ቤቶሙ ፡ ንዑአ ፡ ኦአኃው ፡ ፍቁራን ፡ ወፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፤ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወአምኅዎ ፡
"Father, there are two young men who are at the door and say they have a necessity to discuss with the abbot. They have a good appearance and affable is their speech, the spirit of wisdom dwells in them and they seem like sons of nobles." The abbot said to the guest-master: "Call them immediately and have them enter to me." He went and according to the custom of their house, addressed them with a joyful spirit, with a radiant face, and with a good word. He said to them: "Come in, brothers beloved and dear to Christ." He introduced them to the abbot and they bowed before him and greeted him.
ወተዜያነዎሙ፡ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ሠናያን ፡ ወራዙት ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ውሉድየ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለምጽአትክሙ ፡ ሐዌሳ” ፡ ለእትወትክሙ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ሰላምከ" ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ዳኅንኑ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ድናን ፡ ንሕነ ፤ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እምአይ ፡ ብሔር ፡ አንትሙ ። ወይ ቤልዎ ፡ እምርጐቅ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ንሕነ ፤ እምብሔረ ፡ ቡር ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ በአይ ፡ ምክን ያት ፡ መጸእክሙ ፡ ወምንት ፡ ማኅሠሥ” ፡ ብክሙ ፡ ወምንተ ፡ መፍቅድክሙ ፡ ኦው ሉድየ ። ወአውሥእዎ ፡ ሠናያን ፡ ወራዙት ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንፈቅድ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ምንኩ ስና ፡ ወአውሥአ ፡ አብ ፡፣ መንፈሳዊ ፣፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ምንኩስናሰ ፡ አኮ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ በጉጐዓ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ጽንዕት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መቃዓን ፤ ወመጽብብ ፡ ፍኖታ ። ወአውሥእአዎ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሠናያን ፡ ወራዙት ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ጥቡዓን ፡ ንሕነ ፤ ለሥርዓተ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ምንተ ፡ ይበቀዓነ ፡ ንብረተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ እመ ፡ ረከብ ናሁ ፡ ያሐልፈነ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኃጣእናሁ ፡ ረባሐቲሁ ፡ ያኃዝነነ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ በመንፈሱ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለዓቃቢፆ ፡ ገበዝ ፡ ሖር ፡ አምጽእ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አበው ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ዓቃቢ ፡ ገበዝ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መዛግብት ፡ ወአምጽእአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፤
12 The abbot conversed with those good young men and said to them: "My little children, peace to your coming, welcome!" They answered him: "Your peace be with us, venerable father!" He said to them: "Is (everything) well?" They answered him: "In our Lord Jesus Christ, we are well." He said to them: "From which country are you?" They answered him: "We come from a distant country, from the country of Bur." He said to them: "For what reason have you come, what do you seek and what is your desire, my little children?" The good young men answered him saying: "We want to (embrace) the monastic life." The spiritual father answered them and said: "One cannot embrace the monastic life in a hurry. It is in fact hard, narrow and difficult is its way (Mt 7:14)." Those good young men answered him and said to him: "We are decided to (live) the monastic order. What good are the possessions of this world to us? If we conquer them they will escape us and we will be saddened if we do not obtain their advantages." Having heard this, the abbot rejoiced in his spirit and said to the keeper "Go and bring me 'the words of the Fathers'." He went to the hall of writings, took "the words of the Fathers" and brought it to the abbot in the presence of the two young men who were still with him.

13 (The abbot) opened the book before them and having opened the book he found the point where it says: "The most important wealth of a monk is poverty together with humility, patience, and the fear of God." And again, where it says: "The glory of the monk is when his words match his work." He taught them these and many other similar things which penetrated immediately into the sea of their heart, like fresh water that refreshes the mouth of a thirsty person. And they accepted them because they had found what they had longed for and admired the beauty of the old man's discourse which gladdened the heart like sipping wine (Ps 103:15). They lived therefore fasting and praying, according to the monastic practice while (the monks) put them to the test to understand what their intention was. After a few days they recognized their goodness and virtue as well as their fervent zeal. The monks of the place said to the abbot: "Our father, how beautiful is their path, their behavior, their

obedience, their patience in trials! They are truly called and fit for the monastic life." The abbot rejoiced at these words and thanked the Lord of heaven who grants penitents (the grace) to begin and leads them to perfection (Phil 1:6). To Him be glory forever and ever!

### Monastic profession of Abuna Demyanos

14 After that, those young men presented themselves to the abbot and said to him: "Father, we want you to clothe us in the monastic habit and make us monks." He replied immediately and said to them: "My children, [it is well! I will do according to your desire and may the Lord make you worthy to embrace this state of life and fulfill in you the hope He gave me when He manifested Himself saying: 'Through those who come from the East I will build your house'." He then went to the church and summoned the priests, the deacons, and the monks. He brought the book of the Commandment of the monks and the priest read, saying: "My brother, for what reason have you come and are you prostrating yourself before the altar and the Holy Church?". He answered him: "(Because) I have chosen the monastic life, O venerable father!". He said to him: "Do you wish to take upon yourself the holy askema of the angels and join the community of monks?". He replied: "Certainly, reverend father". He said to him: "You have made a truly good choice if you carry out good works; since the works of righteousness are conquered with toil, with truth, and with commitment, choose to offer your submission to God".
ፅር ፡ ኅረይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታብእ ፡ ቅኔከ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወይቤ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ በረድኤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ክቡር ። ኢይኩን ፡ እምዕዳ ፡ ወእምዓፀባ ፡ ዘጐየይከ ። 'ወይቤ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አባ ፣ ክቡር፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ተኃድግኑ ፡ ዓለመ ፤ ወዙሎ” ፡ ዘውስቴታ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እወ ፡ አባ ፡ ክቡር። ተዓቀብኑ፤ ፡ ንጽሕና ፤ በተቀንዮ ፡ ወበተጸምዶ ። ወይቤ ፡ እወ ፡ አባ ፡ ክቡር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ታፈቅርኑ ፡ እግዚአብሔርሃኞ ፡ በአማን ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ አኃዊሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤ ፡-እወ ፡ አባ ፡ ክቡር ። ትትዔገሥኑ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ወኃዘነ ፡ ወሥርዐተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ በእንተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እወ ፡ አባ ፡ ክቡር ። ዑቅ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ አየ፣ ኪዳነ ፡ ዘትሁቦ ፡ ለእግዚእከ ፡ ወአምላክከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ 'ይጽሕፉ ፡ መላእክት” ፡ ተአምኖተከ ፡ ወየኃሥጮሥ” ፡ እምኔከ ፡ አመ ፡ ዳግም ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወፈጺሞ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ሰመየ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ለድምያኖስ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ናሁ ፡እነግረከ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትመስል ፡
15 He said: "So be it, with the help of God, venerable father!". "Is it not that you have fled from debts and from the harshness (of life)?". [ He answered: "No, venerable Father!" He said to him: "Do you renounce the world and all that it contains, as Our Lord has commanded?". He answered: "Yes, venerable father!". He said to him: "Will you adhere to purity in submission and fasting?". He answered: "Yes, venerable father!". He said to him: "Will you truly love the Lord and all the brothers of Christ?". He answered: "Yes, venerable father!". (He said to him:) "Will you endure toil, pain, and the monastic lifestyle for the kingdom of heaven?" He answered: "Yes, venerable father!". (He said:) "My son, pay attention to the kind of promise you are making to your Lord and God. For now, the angels are writing the (act of your) commitment, for which they will ask you to render an account at the second coming of Our Lord Jesus Christ". Once the ceremony of monastic investiture was finished, he gave him the name Damyanos. The abbot said to Damyanos his son: "Behold, I show you the perfect rule that resembles the rule of Christ. Therefore, I show you the virtues that you must perform and keep and

all the evils from which you must flee by abandoning the world. There is nothing that surpasses the hope of crucifixion and death. Know, my son, that you must be crucified and dead to the world and the world to you (Gal 6:14)"

16 Our father Damyanos said to his master: "I accept everything you tell me, venerable father! I repudiate my father and my mother, my brothers and my sisters, my wife and my companions, my friends and my beloved ones, all the riches of the world that give luster to the flesh. I renounce (all this) for the sake of Christ (Mt 19:29 //). I want, in fact, to follow Him and carry His yoke (Mt 11:30) as He Himself says: 'Whoever wants to join me, let him hate his soul, carry the cross of his death, and follow me' (cf. Mk 8:34). I desire this word, so that I may have communion with those who observe it; as for its implementation, it will be He who will allow me to realize it. For He said: 'Seek and you will find, ask and you will obtain, knock and it will be opened to you. For everyone who asks will have, and whoever seeks will find, to him who knocks it will be opened' (Mt 7:7-8). I too beseech Him to give me the strength to carry out what I have begun so that I may please Him. For He said: 'Without me there is nothing you can do. Nothing at all' (Jn 15:5). And I have the hope of sanctifying myself by following the yearning of my heart as the word of the blessed Apostle, our father Peter, enlightened me in his first epistle when he says: 'And the Lord who has called you in all glory for having suffered a little, He Himself will bring you to perfection and will give you wisdom and will strengthen you, He to whom be glory for ever and ever! Amen' (1Pt 5:10-11)"

### Spiritual journey of Abuna Demyanos
ወአኀዘ ፡ ይትመሀር ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወዜናሆሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ይውጥን፤ ፡ ወያጥሪ ፡ ጊሩታተ ፡ ዘያርጐ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። አጥ ረየ ፡ የውሃተ ፡ ወንጽሐ ፡ ልብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሕያዋን ። አጥረየ ፡ ተዓግሦ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘበጽሖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጥርያ ፡ ለነፍሱ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ወበትዕግሥትክሙ ፡ ታጠርይዋ ፡ ለነፍስክሙ ። አጥረየ ፡ ኅድአተ" ፡ ወአርምሞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ማኅደረ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ፈቀደ ፡ ይኩን ፡ መሐሬ?" ፡ ከመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ዘበ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኩኑ ፡ መሐርያነ" ፡ ከመ ፡ አቡክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ መሐሪ ፡ ውእቱ ። ተግኅሠ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ጽልእ ፡ ወመዓት ፡ ትዕቢት ፡ ወቅንአት ፡ እንተ ፡ ታለብስ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወታወርድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። አጥረየ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ገድላተ ፡
17 He then began to study the book of the Prophets and the message of the Apostles. And he began to enrich himself with the goodness that would open the door of the kingdom of heaven to him. He acquired meekness and purity of heart (Mt 5:8) to inherit the land of the living. He acquired patience in everything that happened to him so that he could acquire his soul, as Our Lord said in the Gospel: "By your patience you will acquire your souls" (Lk 21:18). He enriched himself with stillness and silence to become a dwelling place of the Holy Spirit. He wanted to be merciful as his Father who is in heaven, because He said: "Be merciful as your Father is merciful" (Lk 6:36). He separated himself from everything that is evil (Job 1:1), from hatred, from anger, from pride, and from envy, which makes one clothed in death and makes one descend to the underworld. He accumulated many merits, according to the measure granted to him by the Holy Spirit

(1Cor 12:7; Heb 2:4) which in the first place strengthens and then brings to fullness (Phil 1:6) and inspires courage until one obtains the promised reward (Phil 3:13). 18 He therefore performed prostrations day and night. Then, having finished the number of prostrations that he performed like (if it were) a wheel, he would take a large stone and lift it onto his head and stand where there was no support and sing the Psalms, the Canticles of the Prophets, the Song of Songs, and their other habitual prayers. And to his soul he would say: "Remember the day of your departure from the world and do not forget that covenant that the priests made you swear before the altar of the law of God. O soul, O soul, (remember) the day of fear and wrath, the day of bitterness and groaning. O soul, O soul, remember the day of weeping and tumult, the day of weeping and clamor. O soul, O soul, remember the day of affliction and death, the day of judgment and condemnation and calamity. O soul, O soul, remember the day of thunder and trembling, the day of darkness, of mist and of hail (Ex 19:16). O soul, O soul, remember the day of judgment and retribution when the mother cannot save her children and the earth returns its deposit. 19 "O soul, O soul, remember the day of toil and altercation, the day of terror and flight. O soul, O soul, remember the terrible and frightening day of judgment. Terrible for sinners, (a day) of joy for the righteous, of mourning for the foolish and of gladness for the wise (Mt 25). It is (a day) of mourning for the proud, of joy for the humble. It is mourning for the stubborn, gladness for the meek. It is (a day) of pain for the rapacious. O soul, O soul, remember the reward for the righteous, for the elect, and for the victorious martyrs. O soul, O soul, remember the delight of the penitent monks and of the patient virgins in various types of trials: every kind of tasty fruit, every kind of perfume and aroma of the paradise of delight, paradise of joy that (God) has prepared as an inheritance for those who fear Him". And while he was saying this, his tears flowed like water. Furthermore, when he did the prostrations, he bathed the earth with the drops of his sweat. And while he was exercising himself in (all) this, he did not want it to become public knowledge, but did everything in secret (Mt 6:5-6) and was meek like children (Mt 18:3-4; 1Pt 2:2).


[p. 152] {#po.50.p152}

### Abuna Demyanos is elected steward of Dabra Salam
ወርእዮሙ ፡ አኃው ፡ ሥነ ፡ ግፅዙ ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ዝሰ ፣ መነኮስ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትረስዮ ፡ መጋቢ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርኢነ ፡ ሞገስ" ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዓውዶ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ናነክር ፡ ጥበቢሁ ፡ ወዕቃቤ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ወይዋሄሁ ፡ ወጣዕመ ፡ ነገሩ ፡፤ ወአፍቅሮቱ ፡ ነግደ ፣፡ ወኩሎ" ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ፍናዊሁ፤ ፡ መፍትው ፡ ለርእይ ፤ ወአዳም ፡ ለሰሚዕ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ጫምዎ ፡ መጋቤ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መሥመሬ'፡ ፡ አኀው ፡ ወለመምህሩኒ ።
20 Seeing the goodness of his behavior, the brothers said to the abbot, who was our father Muse: "O father, it is good that you appoint this monk as steward. We have in fact observed that he is surrounded by grace and we greatly admire his wisdom, his firm and decisive word (Ps 111:6), his meekness and the goodness of his speech, his love for guests and for all people. All his ways are pleasant to the sight and delightful to hear". And after a few days they elected our father Damyanos as steward, and he pleased the brothers and his master.

### Abuna Demyanos continues his path of asceticism among trials

21 Again he began to reflect and said: "What is better for me to do?". And he began to chastise his flesh so that his soul might be saved. In fact, Abba Anthony in the title of the book exhorts us saying: "If we suffer hunger and thirst, nakedness, suffering, if we exercise ourselves (in all this) with conviction, we will be worthy of the kingdom of heaven. Let us prefer suffering in order to be able to find God. Let us despise our flesh so that our soul may be saved". He therefore began to abstain from foods with salt and from drinking the beer that gladdens the heart (Ps 103:15). He lived in this manner and in the fear of God for two years, without anyone becoming aware of this conduct of his assumed for the Lord. One day our father Muse summoned his son, our father Demyanos, and said to him: "My son, bring me beer so that I may drink and my soul may bless you!". He went and promptly brought it. He said to him: "Drink for love!". Our father Damyanos smiled and said to him: "Father, forgive me because I have not tasted this for a long time and no one had noticed it except you today, O venerable father". He said this to him because of the slander that Satan had stirred up against him through his brothers. He is in fact the adversary (1Pt 5:8) from the beginning (Jn 8:44) and never allows, not even for a moment, that the saints of the Most High have peace, but only pain, until they defeat him with the dew of mercy that descends from the Lord (Is 26:19). And as soon as they defeat him, they say: "Where is your sting, O death?" (1Cor 15:55). Death has in fact been defeated and suppressed and weakness has been swallowed up by strength. "Praise be to God who has granted us to win through Jesus Christ Our Lord" (1Cor 15:57). To Him be glory together with His Father and the Holy Spirit. Amen. 22 Let us therefore resume the thread of the discourse. Once he had heard his son and our father Damyanos, our father Muse denounced the malice of Satan who had provoked suspicion towards that valiant man of God and

said to his son: "May the Lord strengthen you and bring you to perfection. Amen". He then let him go and our father Damyanos lived by subjugating his flesh, mindful of what the Apostle had said: "Our momentary suffering will procure us honor and glory" (2Cor 4:17). Therefore, he lived in fasting and prayer, in hunger, in nakedness, and in indigence, remembering what Our Lord says in the Gospel: "Blessed are the poor in spirit for theirs is the kingdom of heaven" (Mt 5:3). He groaned and wept because He says: "Blessed are those who now weep for they shall rejoice" (Mt 5:4). He hungered for righteousness because He says: "Blessed are those who hunger for righteousness for they shall be satisfied" (Mt 5:6). He acquired purity of heart because He says: "Blessed are the pure [of heart for they shall see God" (Mt 5:8). He became a peacemaker because He says: "Blessed are the peacemakers for they shall be called children of God" (Mt 5:9).

23 And he walked in the ways of God as David says: "Blessed are the pure in their way and those who walk in the law of the Lord. Blessed are those who seek His testimony and who seek it with all their heart" (Ps 118:1-2). Again he says: "Blessed are all those who fear the Lord and those who walk in the ways of the Lord" (Ps 127:1). And the offspring in Zion as Isaiah says: "Blessed is he who has offspring in Zion and houses in Jerusalem" (Is 31:9). He purified his flesh from the impurity of this world, remembering what the Apocalypse says: "Blessed are those who have purified their robes with the blood of the Lamb, because they will be able to have authority over the tree of life" (Rev 7:14 and 22:14). He enriched himself with wisdom and intelligence according to what Jesus [son of Eleazar of Jerusalem (Sir 50:27) had taught him when he said: "Say to wisdom, 'You are my sister!', and make knowledge your friend" (Pr 7:14).
አጥረየ ፡ ተፋቅሮ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በከመ ፡ መሀሮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ዕፍረት ፡እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እመኒ ፡ አእመርኩ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በሐውርት ፡ ወነገረ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ እጓለ ፤ እምሕያው” ፡ ወነገረ ፡ መላእክት ፤ ወተፋቅሮ ፡ አልብየ ። ኮንኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ድምፀ ፡ ብርት ፡ ወእመ ፡ አኮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዶል ፡ ዘይነቁ"፤ ። ወእመኒ ፡ ተነበይኩ ፡ ወአእ መርኩ ፡፤ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኅቡእ” ፣ ወኩሎ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ብየ ፡ ሃይማኖት" ፡ እስከ ፡ አፈልስ ፤ አድባረ” ፡ ወተፋቅሮ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ከንቶ ፡ ኮንኩ ። ወእመኒ ፡፤ ወሀብኩ ፡፤ ምጽዋተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋይየ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ሥጋየኒ ፡ ለእመ ፡ አብላዕኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትመዘገን ወአልብየ ፡ ተፋቅሮ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘበቋዕኩ ። ተፋቅሮ ፡ ያስተዔግሥ ፡ ተፋቅሮ ፡ ያስተ
24 He acquired love with everyone as our father Paul "of the perfumed speech" had taught him when he says: "Even if I knew the languages of all countries and the languages of every man and the languages of the angels but did not have love, I would become like the sound of bronze or like a bell that rings (in vain). Even if I prophesied and knew every secret and all wisdom; even if I had a faith to move mountains and did not have love, I would have become useless. Even if I gave all my resources as alms and if I also offered my flesh as food to be thanked for it and did not have love, it would profit me nothing. Love makes one patient, love makes one forgive. Love does not allow one to be envious.


[p. 156] {#po.50.p156}
ምሕር ። ተፋቅሮ ፡ ኢያስተቃንእ ፡ ተፋቅሮ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ ተድላ ፡ ለርእሱ ፡ ለባሕ ቲቱ ። ተፋቅሮ ፡ ኢያስተምፅዕዕ ፡ ወኢያሔሊ ፡ እኩየ ፡ 'ወኢያሜክር ፡ እኩየዎ ፡ ወያ ስተፌሥሕ ፡ በጽድቅ ። ተፋቅሮ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ ወይስተዔግሥ ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ ያስተዌክል ። ተፋቅሮ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ፡ ኢይኃሥር ፡ ወኢያወድቅ ፡
Love does not desire joy solely for itself. Love does not make one angry and does not make one think evil, it does not advise evil but makes one rejoice in righteousness. Love makes one forgive in every circumstance and makes one patient and in everything makes one hope. Love will not degrade forever nor does it debase" (1Cor 13:1-8)

### Summary
ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አጥረየ ፣ ወዓቀቦ ፡ መዓልተ ፤ ወሌሊተ ። ወአግዘዘ”' ፡ ዓፀዶ ፡ በእብነ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይባእ ፡ ሰራቂ ፡ ወኢይሥርቅ ፡ ሠራቂ ፡ ወርቆ ። በከመ ፡ ሠረቆ ፡ ለቀዳማይ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እምገነተ ፡፤ ተድላ ፡ ወአውረሶ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሦክ ፡ ወአሜ ከለ” ። አግዘዘ ፡ ዓፀዶ ፡ በእብነ ፣ ኩኩሕ ፡ ዘንቤ” ፡ ዓፀድሰ"፤ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ልብ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወእብነ ፡ ኩኩሕኒ ፡ ዘንቤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘተሳረረት ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሐብተ” ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ዘተወፈያ ፡ እምእደ ፡ ኩኩሕ ።
25 He acquired all this and kept it day and night. And he barred his courtyard with a boulder so that the thief would not enter and steal his treasure (Mt 5:19-21) in the same manner with which he took the first man from the paradise of delight (Gen 3) and gave him the land of thorns and thistles as an inheritance (Gen 3:18). We have said that he defended his courtyard with a mass of rock, to indicate the door of the heart. What we have called "mass of rock" is Christ upon whom the Church was founded, whose guardian is our Father Peter who received it from the hand of the Rock (Mt 16:18-19)

### Abuna Demyanos receives holy orders
ንትመየጥኬ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ጾታ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘትሩፈ ፡ ምግባር ፡ ማር ፡ አቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ብእሴ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ቤት ፤ አሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ወለመምህሩኒ ፤ ወተሠይመ ፡ ሚመተ ፡ ዲይቀቶ'ና ። ወአሥመሮሙ ፡፣ ለካህናት ፡ ወለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ፣ በመስተፋጠኒት ፤ መልእክቱ" ። ወካዕበ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትለአክ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ንዝህ ላል ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ዕፍረት ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ በመልእክቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ኢታስትት ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ዘላዕሌከ ። ተልእከ ፡ ወኢያስተተ ፡ ተግባሮ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይተግህ ፡ በምህሮ"” ፡ ቃል ፡ ወኢያብደረ ፡ ረባሐ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ እስመ ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ኅሊናሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ኪዳን ፡ በሰማይ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ልብክሙ ። ወበወንጌልኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዝግቡ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ መዝገበ ፡ ዘበ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ መዝገብክሙ ፡ህየ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ልብክሙ። ዘንተኬ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ማር ፡ አቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ አልዓለ ፡ ኅሊናሁ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ሉዓሌ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴፎ ፡ ክላሌ ፡ አርትዓ ፡ ትንባሌ ፡ ዓቂቦ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ፲ ፡ እለ ፡ ጽሑፋት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ጽሌ ፡ በረከቱ ፡ ትዕቀበነ” ፡ አሜን" ። ወዓዲ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ለቅዳሴ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ፪ዲያቆ ናት ፡ በየማኑ ፡ ወ በፀጋሙ ፡ እለ ፡ የኃሥጮሠ ፡ አናብዐ ፡ እምአዕይንቲሁ ፡ እስመ ፡
26 Let us therefore resume the thread of the discourse of the one abounding in virtue, Mar our father Damyanos, man of God. While he was engaged in the service of the house, he pleased the Lord and his master and was conferred the order of the diaconate. With his solicitous commitment he did what was pleasing to the priests and to the Lord his God. Later he was ordained a priest and gladdened the Lord by serving without negligence and putting into practice the word of our father Paul "of the perfumed speech" who says in the letter to our father Timothy: "Do not neglect the grace that is upon you" (1Tm 4:14). He committed himself and did not neglect his duties, taking care in the teaching of the Word (Acts 6:2). He did not pursue the advantages of this world because he had placed his heart where Christ is (Col 3:1-2). In fact, our Lord Himself, in the Book of the Testament, said: "Let your heart be in heaven". Also in the Gospel He says: "Make yourselves a treasure in heaven and where your treasure is, there will your heart be" (Mt 6:20-21). 27 Aware therefore of this, Mar, our father Damyanos raised his thought to the most sublime heights, hopeful of coronation, he pursued intercession, keeping the ten words of justice, written on the tablet (Ex 34:28). May his blessing keep us Amen! Regarding this saint, it is also said that when he stood for the Eucharistic celebration, two deacons stood beside him who wiped the tears from his eyes: one stood at his right and one at his left.


[p. 158] {#po.50.p158}
ይውኅዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወይከድን ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወያርኅስ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡፤ ወዓዲ ፡ ምድርኒ ፡ ያርሕስ ፡ በአንብዑ ።
In fact (the tears) flowed like a spring of water and covered his eyes, bathed his vestments, and he also watered the earth with his tears.

### Abuna Demyanos plans to go on a mission

28 After many days and long years, the Spirit of God came upon him (Lk 4:18a) which made him reflect thus: "Does Paul not say: 'Do not strive only for yourselves, but (also) for your neighbor?' (1Cor 10:24, 33; Phil 2:4), you too have zeal to save people. For it says: 'Love your neighbor as yourself' (Mt 19:19)". For this reason he went to our father Muse and said to him: "O venerable father, if I have found grace before your eyes, allow me to live alone in my dwelling together with those who share my thought and participate in my lifestyle. The community order is in fact for the strong and not for the weak, while for the weak it is more convenient to withdraw!". The venerable father Abba Muse answered him and said to him: "Very well!". When he gave him his assent, he prostrated himself before his feet and said to him: "Then, bless me O father, with a perfect blessing!". 29 The elder opened his mouth and said: "The blessing of Abraham that had dwelt in Isaac and the blessing of Isaac that rested upon Jacob and the blessing of Jacob that was divided among the twelve sons of Israel rest upon you. Amen! O my son Damyanos, my friend and confidant (Ps 54:14), beloved of my soul! The blessing of our father Elijah that abounded in our father Elisha, abound in you. The blessing of my fathers, oppressed by the world, of Abba Ewostatewos the pilgrim and of Abba Absadi, rest upon you. Amen! 'Blessed shall you be in the city and blessed in the country! Blessed shall you be when you come in and when you go out' (Dt 28:3, 6). Amen! And the blessing of me, a sinner, manifest itself in you and be for you grace wherever you go!". Digression

30 And Abba Muse remembered the word of the man of God who came to him when he was mourning the death of his children and he shut himself in his cell and began to pray with all his heart to know how the rest of those who were already sleeping was, and the fate of those who were (still) alive, and what would happen later. And a man of God was sent to him, one of those who lived in the hermitage, he addressed him with the word of God and said to him: "Why do you grieve for the death of your children? I have in fact chosen them myself for my kingdom; as for your house, I will build it through those who will come from the east and your memory will be raised up through them!". And Abba Muse said: "There is no thing that is impossible for God, may God do according to His will (Lk 1:37-38), to Him be glory. Amen!".

### Abuna Demyanos takes leave of Abuna Muse and wanders from one place to another
ንግባእኬ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ ዘንቤ ። ንቤሎሙ ፡ ለአኃዊሁ ፡ ምንተኬ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ። ይቤሎሙ”! ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሐውጽ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡
31 And when he remembered this word, Abba Muse said to Abba Damyanos: "My son, (do not) delay your visit to me so that we can discuss all the needs of the flesh and of the soul!". Abuna Damyanos answered Abuna Muse: "Certainly, O father, I agree with everything you say!". He took leave of him and set out on his way. And some blessed brothers who shared his lifestyle followed him, and they said to one another: "We too will follow you and will not abandon you, whether for death or for life, we are resolved (to follow you) with all our heart and with all our mind" (Ruth 1:16-17; Jn 11:16). They were: Abba Qozmos and Abba Petros, the deacon Abimos and the cantor Margorawos, the deacon Istifanos and their son Tasfa Iyasus. They established a good order, pleasing to the Lord, because it says: "If two or three join together in my name, I will be in their midst" (Mt 18:20). It also says: "Whoever loves me, let him observe my word! My Father will love him, we will come to him and rest (with him) and together we will dwell with him" (Jn 14:23). 32 They therefore agreed on the divine order that is "more lovable than gold and topaz, sweeter than honey and the honeycomb (Ps 18:10). And they chose [ as superior Abba Damyanos. And they passed on and arrived at one of the desert places of Baraka and lived there for a few years and not many. They left that place and arrived at one of the monasteries of Sarawe by the name of May Sagla and lived in it performing works of righteousness, for a little and not a long time. They moved from there, passed on, and returned to the hermitage of Baraka and chose a mountain. They agreed to live on it, those shining stars of the spring season that the veil of sin, namely the pleasure of concupiscence, pride, envy, and every work of impurity that strikes the souls, had not darkened. O bright stars that the Sun of righteousness has made shine with its radiant beauty, wait therefore until with the help of your prayer, I tell the experience of your life, and the beauty of your grace. Abuna Demyanos visits the monastery of Abuna Sem'on 33 Let us resume telling the facts of the elder of whom we have already spoken. Let us tell his brothers what he said to them. He said to his

brothers: "I desire to visit the monastery of Abba Sam'on. The book says in fact that whoever approaches the saints will become holy (Lev 10:3) and he who listens to their words will find grace". Those blessed (men) answered him in chorus and said to him: "So be it, O our Father! Let it be as you say". He therefore took leave of them and went, and arrived at the monastery of the man of God, Abuna Sem'on. When Abba Sam'on noticed our father Damyanos from afar, he saw the beneficial grace that dwelt in him and said to those who were with him: "How much grace surrounds this priest! In fact, he gathers many souls with his word". And when he approached him, he rose and welcomed him, kissed him with a spiritual kiss (1Cor 16:20) and said to him: "Welcome! How are you, O venerable one of Bur?". Our father Damyanos said to him: "Venerable are rather those who do the will of God and observe it" (Mt 12:50). After having said this, they began to converse among themselves about the great things that God had done (Lk 1:49), about the various experiences in the ascetic life, about the calling of the nations by means of the evangelical proclamation, about wandering among the (various) countries and villages, about swimming in the sea of the world to fish for souls (Mt 4:19) from the abyss of sin. 34 About these things he questioned Mar Sem'on, seer of hidden things and miracle worker, (a man) in whom the grace of God dwelt. Mar Sam'on said to our father Damyanos: "You have spoken well and I liked everything you told me because everything you expressed is divine word, everything (you uttered) is for God and all, from beginning to end, is just. And I will announce to you what the Lord has revealed to me. And He did not reveal it to me by merit of my righteousness but because He desired your good and the good of all those who call upon you and believe in you and in the word of your evangelical preaching. You will in fact have to become a preacher and teacher of the nations that are thirsty to hear the word of the Lord" (Am 8:11). Digression I: on idolatry 35 There were in fact among them those who divined through birds, and those who made omens through the movement of the eyes. There were others who prostrated themselves before stones. There were those who poured libations on the heights and who sacrificed to demons saying: "Help us, O sons of our lords!" And they poured blood on the edge of the precipice. And they did not break the bone of their sacrificial offering (Ex 12:46; Num 9:12; Jn 19:36). And when they rose to return (home?) they did not turn nor

ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ልማደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይጥዕሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ያውጽሑ ፡ ወይንዝተሩ ፡ እመኒ ፡ ዘመብልዕ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ዘመስቴ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ዘጸብሕ ። ወቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ እለ ይመልኡ ፡ መብልዐ" ፡ በጋሕፈት ፡ ወአው ፡ በመጽብሕ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ በመቅጹት ፡ ወእመኒ ፡፤ በዘኮነ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ወያነብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀልቀል ፡ ወየኃድግዎጆ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትሐዘቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበልዑ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወያአትዉ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ንስ ቲት ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ቋዓት ፤ ወይበልዕዎ ፤ ወኢያተርፍዎ ። ወይትፌሥሑ ፡ በእን ተዝ ፡ ነገር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመስሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ በልዑ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወያአብዱ ፡ ለሊሆሙ” ፡ ወባዕዳንሂ ፤ ያአብዱ” ፡ በእበዶሙ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጠዋይ ፡ ትጠዊ።
look back. And there were those who had the habit of not tasting (anything) before having poured the libation or without having sprinkled, both the foods and the drinks or the dishes. There were among them those who poured the foods into baskets or into bowls, or into pots, or into any other object and placed everything on the precipice and left it there observing if they ate it, then they returned to their homes. After a short period of time the crows would come and consume it (Gen 15:11; Jub 11:37) without leaving remnants. The offerers rejoiced at this thing thinking that (the crows) had eaten for them and in this way they became foolish and with their foolishness they made others senseless too. As David says: "With the crooked you will be crooked" (Ps 17:26).

36 About them and those like them he says again: "Even though he is noble, man has not been able to recognize this. He has become like the animal that has no sense and has ended up resembling it. In their actions they have truly become like animals and have forgotten the God who created them because they have failed in what He taught them" (Ps 48:13). In fact, without the book and without instruction, man cannot know anything. And for this very reason the books have told us that the Lord in various epochs has sent the prophets (Heb 1:1) who proclaim the glad tidings (Is 52:7; Rom 10:15) and who teach the worship of the Lord and lastly (cf. Heb 1:2) He came (Himself) for the salvation of the world (Jn 3:16-17). As He said to Ezra when he asked Him: "Through whom will you visit your world?" (4Esd 5:56). And (the Lord) said to him: "First of all, through the Son of man and then I myself" (4Esd 6:1). "First of all, through the Son of man", concerns the coming of the Son who became human from Mary, the doubly Virgin. He said: "Then I myself", referring to His second coming to judge sinners and to reward the righteous and to render to each according to his work (Mt 25:31-46; Rom 14:10). To Him be glory for ever! Amen.

37 Let us therefore begin to speak of the coming of our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ in the humanization from the doubly Virgin Mary, for the salvation of the world. For His goodness and for His love towards man (Jn 3:16) He clothed Himself in human flesh, He performed the human work except for sin (Heb 4:15). He suffered hunger (Mt 4:2) and thirst (Jn 19:28), He sweated (Lk 22:44) and He felt toil (Jn 4:6) and He arrived until He died (Phil 2:8). He died and with His death He defeated the angel of death (Heb 2:14-15). He destroyed the perfidy of Satan, He rose from the dead and He freed the souls of the righteous. He spent forty days and forty nights

teaching the Apostles (Acts 1:3). After that He ascended to the heavens, giving His Apostles the order that said: "But you remain in the city of Jerusalem until you are clothed with power from on high" (Acts 1:4). Our Fathers the Apostles remained (there) awaiting the promise of the Father. After that, on the tenth day, on the day of the first Sabbath, at the third hour, the Holy Spirit came upon them like tongues of fire and the strong Spirit was poured out upon their mouths (Acts 2:3-4). And with this Spirit of power that sustained them wherever they went, they gave themselves to itinerancy in all countries. For their part, they did not withhold their backs from flagellations (Acts 5:40), their cheeks from slaps, and their throats from the sword. And despite all these tribulations, they called and gathered the souls so that they could come to the knowledge of what is good and to the right faith, while the same Spirit of power
strengthened them. To Him be glory for ever! Amen.

### Abuna Sam'on encourages and blesses Abuna Damyanos

38 "And you, my son Damyanos, man of God, to you the blessedness because you remembered that benefit which is the liberation of souls from the darkness of sin for the knowledge of righteousness and because you make of them one flock for one shepherd (Jn 10:16). And may God make you worthy to be able to obtain the wealth of grace, because when a sinner repents and converts, the Lord rejoices more over him than over ninety-nine righteous who have no need of penance (Lk 15:7). And you too, do not stop wandering in the land of Bur, because that is your portion. May the protection of our Father Peter accompany you, may the grace of our father Paul and the help of all the Apostles be with you. Amen!". Abuna Demyanos returns to his monastery 39 When our father Sem'on said this to our father Damyanos, he rejoiced greatly. He rose, received his blessing, took leave of him, kissed him, and set out on his way joyful for all the things that the man of God had told him. He returned to his house where there were those stars who welcomed him with a heart full of joy and with a luminous conscience because it seemed to them that they had not seen him for a long time. Eulogy to the disciples of Abuna Demyanos

as David said in the hallelujah psalm: "He gave them an order and they did not transgress it" (Ps 148:6). O good stars! You too, thus, did not overstep the limit that He established for you. You kept the religion that offers the hope of righteousness, namely the Orthodox Faith. You kept it, you observed it minutely, you preserved its law, you gathered its perfumed aromas, you kept it, you delighted in its goodness. You kept it, you drank from its cisterns, you kept it and you enriched yourselves with its gold. You kept it and you rejoiced for its consolation.

41 O bright stars who have clothed yourselves with the dignity of the angels which is the monastic habit, the sacred scapular, and who have striven to multiply the talents (Mt 25:14ff) like those servants who await the coming of their lord and who keep watch both in the second and in the third hour of the night so that upon his arrival he finds them vigilant (Lk 12:36-37) and when he knocks they open to him. And you too have made yourselves similar to the behavior of those elect servants who were praised in the Gospel (Mt 24:45-46). You have acquired patience together with the fear of God. You have pursued a good battle (2Tm 4:6) together with good work. You have accumulated goodness together with love. You have procured humility together with mercy. You have acquired faith and trust in the Lord. You have cultivated the contempt of the values of the world and you have reached perfection. You have put your hand to the handles of the plow, you have taken the plowshare and you have not turned back (Lk 9:62) so as not to distort your furrow. You took baskets and sowed grains of goodness and watched over them so that the birds would not devour them (Mk 4:3-4) and the wild boar would not contaminate them (Ps 79:14) which represent the spirits of the demons who do not want anyone of the race of the children of men to be saved (Lk 8:12).

42 If man does not prevent them from opening his doors so that they enter into the sea of his heart (Mt 8:28-32), they will swim there, they will dance there, they will agitate it and will lead it wherever they want. You instead, O bright stars, burned with the flame of faith and the fire of your mouth consumed them. You cast your hand like a sling, making the sign of the cross and you struck them until (making them) fall, leaning on the help of the Holy Spirit. O pearls that shine in the square of the assembly, you are an ornament of the king's house. O young men, girded with the shroud of the Trinity! O combatants who have stretched the bow of the cross towards the enemy! Blessed is the womb that bore you, blessed are the breasts that have nursed you (Lk 11:27)! its fruits are delicious like honey. She is truly a blessed woman and glorious is the womb that conceived those rings, through a legitimate marital relationship. 43 This woman is truly blessed and blessed are the breasts that raised them (as) sentinels. That woman is truly blessed who made these sprouts grow with the word that educated their flesh and their soul and that built them up and introduced them into the munificent atrium. Sosenna! daughter of the elect, daughter of nobles, daughter of the righteous and of the meek. I cannot express a praise of you to perfection, nor weave the praise of your children! I have said only a little while your merits are very numerous. I am in fact poor as to knowledge, nor do I possess capacity in distinguishing things and in rectifying letters. I would only like this (writing) to be acceptable before you like the evening incense (Ex 30:8; Ps 140:2) and like the evening sacrifice of Elijah (1Kgs 18:29) like the two coins of the widow (Lk 21:2) of which He ordered that memory be made in every Eucharistic celebration and in every prayer, so that I may obtain a reward for your prayer; establish therefore a covenant together with your Most High (God). To Him be glory for ever. Amen!

44 Let us return therefore to the facts concerning the man of God. When our father Damyanos returned from the monastery of our father Sam'on, he remained in his dwelling in fasting, in prayer, beseeching and prostrating himself with great diligence, together with hot tears, day and night (Ps 41:4), in the same manner with which our fathers the Apostles fasted and prayed thanking the Holy Spirit before going out for preaching. Having finished the period of his prayer, he wanted to go to the land of Bur as our father Sem'on had indicated to him. He went and arrived at Hagara Masqal, he gathered men and women, the young men and the virgins, the servant and the master, the servants and the ladies, the shepherds left their pastures and came to listen to him and they became a great assembly. 45 He therefore opened his mouth and taught them (Mt 5:2) the word of the Prophets and of the Apostles that ravishes the heart and that makes the bones grow green and they remained enraptured in listening to him. In fact, he taught them with a smile on his mouth and with meekness and succeeded in converting many to the right path of penance. And they abandoned their impure behaviors that came from Satan. Some of them repudiated idolatry which is the origin of every sin, others stopped consulting the fortune-teller, others from making omens through birds and other animals, others stopped killing people and swearing falsely, others


[p. 172] {#po.50.p172}
ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕግ ። ወዝኩሉ ፡ ኀደጉ ፡ ወዘይመስሎ ፤ ወተመይጡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ርትዕት ፣፤ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወአእምሮ ፡ ፍጽምት ፡ እንተ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወወፅአ ፡ ስሙዓተ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ወአድያመ ፡ ቡር።
they ceased to steal and to enter into illegitimate marriages. They renounced all these things and others similar to them and returned to the straight faith and to the perfect knowledge that is in Our Lord Jesus Christ (Col 2:2). And his fame spread in the countries of Bur and in the neighboring zones (Mt 4:23-25).

### Abuna Demyanos the wonderworker
ወይመጽኡ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ በሐውርት ፡ ለአጽምኦቱ ፡ ወይረክቡ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡፤ በቀዔተ ፡ ለድውያንሂ ፡ ይፌውሶሙ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ለምጽ ፡ ያንጽሖሙ ። ወለአጋንንት ፡ ይጌሥዖሙ ፡ ወያወፅኦሙ ፡ በመንፈሰ ፡ አሶት ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ አሶት ፡ ሕዱር" ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። በመንፈሰ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወአሶተ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ተወፈየ”፣ ወሞገሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ተጸገወ ። ወጣዖታተ፡ሰበረ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡መሀረ ፤ ወምሕራማተ ፡ አመዝበረ ። በኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ተፈቅረ ፡ ወበኀበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ተነክረ ፤ ሥነ ፡ ገድል ፡ ተምህረ ፡ ወክበደፆ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ጾረ” ፡ ለዘ ፡ ይረግሞ ፡ ደኃረ ፡ ወጸላእያኒሁ ፡ አግረረ ። በማኅሌተ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘመረ ፡ ወዘይበቀሃ ፡ አእመረ ፡ በእግረ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ሰረረ ፡
46 People from every place flocked to hear him and obtained remedy from him. Indeed, he cured the sick, purified the lepers, rebuked the demons, and cast them out by means of the healing Spirit, because the healing Spirit dwelt in him. He walked in the Spirit of Elijah, received the gift of healing of our father Peter (Acts 5:19; 9:32-34, 36ff), and was invested with the gift of grace of our father Paul (Acts 14:8-10; 19:12). He destroyed idols, taught the faith, and demolished places of worship dedicated to idols. He was loved by his God, his miracles were exalted among men, he was trained to fight the good fight, he endured the burden of daily life, he blessed those who cursed him (Rom 12:14), and he subdued his enemies (Heb 11:33). He sang with canticles of praise, he knew what would benefit him, he flew with the feet of thought to reach the place where he had stored the treasure (Mt 5:19-21) of his submission, he went as far as the seashore, he fished the fish of the sea, he dwelt among the co-heirs (Ps 67:14), and he took pleasure in the ear of justice. [May his blessing remain with us. Amen! Amen!] Abuna Demyanos summoned to heal the bride at the point of death.
መዝገበ ፡ ቅኔሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንበረ ፡ ሐይቀ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወዓሣተ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ አሥገረ ፤ ማእከለ ፡ መዋርስት ፡ ኃደረ ። ኀበ ፡ ሰብለ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ሠምረ” ፡ በረከቱ” ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ፤ ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን” ። ንግባእኬ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዜና ፡ ስብከቱ ፡ ለብፁዕ” ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ለአኩ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እለ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ሀገር ፡ መጠነ ፡ ምክያደ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ነዐአ ፡ ዝየአ ፡ ወኢትጐንዲአ ፡ እስመአ ፡ ንስቲትአ ፡ መርዓትአ ፡ ትደዊአ ። ወአልጸቀትአ ፡ ትሙትአ ። ወባሕቱአ ። ነዓአ ፡ ከመአ ፡ ትደይአ ፡ እዴከአ ፡ ወተሐዩአ ። እስመአ ፡ ኃይልአ ፡ ይወፅእአ ፡ እምኔ ከአ ፡ ወይረድአከአ ። ወተዘከረ ፡ አቡነ ፡፤ ድምያኖስ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘይቤሎሙ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ብክሙ ፡ ሃይማኖት" ፡ መጠነ ፡ ኅጠተ ፡ ሰናፔ ፡ ወእመ ፡ ትቤልዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ፍልስ ፡ እምፈለሰ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ተዘከረ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡፤ ዘየአምን ፡ ብየ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዘአነ ፡ እገብር ፡ ይገብር ፡ ወዘየዐቢ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ይገ
47 Let us therefore treat the history of the preaching of the man of God, Abuna Damyanos. While he was at Hagara Masqal, those of a nearby village that was three hours' walk away sent him messengers, saying: "Come here and do not delay, because there is a young bride who is ill at the point of death. Come, therefore, so that you may lay your hands (upon her) so that she may be healed. Indeed, there is a power that flows from you (Mk 5:30) and that will help you." And our father Domyanos remembered the word of our Lord Jesus Christ who had said to his disciples: "If you have faith as a grain of mustard seed and say to this mountain, 'Move!', it would have moved" (Mt 17:20). He also remembered that he says: "He who believes in me will do

ብር ። ወዝንቱኒ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ፍኖተ ። በከመ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ኢያኢሮስ ፡ አመ ፡ አስተብቀዖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ቤቶ ፣
what I am doing and will do even greater things" (Jn 14:12). And this father of ours, Damyanos, rose immediately with his disciples and undertook that same journey of our Lord, to him be glory, who went to the house of Jairus when he begged him to enter his house (Lk 8:40-56).

48 For he had a two-year-old daughter and she was about to die. Thereafter, while he was going with him, the people were jostling him, and a woman came who had been bleeding for twelve years and had spent all her substance on doctors, and there was no one who had been able to heal her. She approached and stood behind Jesus and touched the fringe of his garment, and immediately her flow of blood stopped. And Jesus said: "Who touched the fringe of my garment?" and everyone denied it. Peter, together with those who were with him, said to him: "Master! Is not a multitude of people pressing against you, and you say 'who touched my garment?'" And the Lord Jesus replied: "There is someone who has touched me, because I know that a power has gone out from me." And when that woman realized that she had not gone unnoticed, she trembled and was afraid, and she prostrated herself before him and told all the people that she had touched (the fringe) and that she had been instantly healed.
ወይቤላ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ እመኒ ፡ ወለትየ ፡ ሃይማኖትኪ ፣ አሕየወተኪ ፡ ሑሪ ፡ በሰላም ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ መጽኡ ፡ እምሰብአ” ፡ ሊቀ ፡ አሕዛብ ፤ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ወለትከሰ ፡ ሞተት ፡ ኢታጻምዎ ፡ ለሊቅ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ለአቡሃ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ዳዕሙ ፡ ተአመን ፡ ተሐዩ ፡ ወለትከሰ ። ወበዊኦ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ከልአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ባዕደ ፡ ኢይባእ' ፡ ኢመኑሂ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ወአቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ። ወእምዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበክይዋ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ፣ ወያስቆቅውዋ ። ወይቤ ሎሙ” ፣ ኢየሱስ ፤ ኢትብክዩ ፡ ኢሞተት ፡ ሕፃን ፡ አላ ፡ ትነውም ። ወሰሐቅዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሞተት ። ወአውዕአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ወአሐዘ ፡ እዴሃ ፡ ወጸውዓ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ተንሥኢ ፡ ወለትየ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ልባ ፡ ወነፍሳ ፡ ወተንሥአት ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ የሀብዋ ፡ ዘትበልዕ ። ወደንገፁ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ፡ ወገሠጾሙ ፡ ኢይንግሩ ፤፡ ወኢለመ ኑሂ ። ዘንተ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን” ። ወዝን ቱኒ ፡ አብ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አቡነ ፣ ድምያኖስ"”' ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፡ ዝነገር ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ ብየ ፡ ግብረፆ ፡ ዘአነ ፡ እገብር ፡ ይገብር ፡ ወዘየዓቢ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ይገብር ። ወተንሚኦ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ አረሞ ፡ አሐዘ ፡ ይሖርፆ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ደብለ ፡ ሱማ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትደዊ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መርዓት ።
49 And the Lord Jesus said to her: "Have faith, my daughter, your faith has healed you. Go in peace!" And while he was speaking with that girl, some (emissaries) of the leader of the people arrived and said to him: "Your daughter is dead, do not disturb the Master!" Having heard (this), the Lord Jesus said to the leader of the people, the father of the child: "Do not fear, only have faith! Your daughter will be healed." And having entered, the Lord Jesus forbade any stranger and anyone else to enter with him except Peter, James, John, and the father and mother (of the sick girl). All the people were weeping and making lamentations over the child. And the Lord Jesus said to them: "Do not weep! The child is not dead but sleeps." And they mocked him because they knew she was dead, and he made everyone go out and took her hand and called her and said to her: "Arise, my daughter!" Then her heart and her soul returned (to her), she arose, and (Jesus) commanded that they give her something to eat. Her father and her mother were frightened, and he warned them not to tell anyone. This did our Lord Jesus, to him be glory for ever and ever. Amen! And in this venerable father, Abuna Damyanos, this word that our Lord had spoken was fulfilled: "He who believes in me will do what I do, and will do even greater things" (Jn 14:12). He then rose from Hagara Masqal, which is called Aramo, and began the journey toward Dabla Suma where that sick bride was.


[p. 176] {#po.50.p176}

### Episode along the way: Abuna Damyanos eliminates an idol

50 Along the way, he arrived at one of the villages of Bur by the name of Dalu'eta. And having arrived there, they told him that in that place there was a stone idol that the infidels worshipped. He said: "Show me that place!", and he wanted to go there, but the people of that bride cried out in a loud voice and, raising a loud lament, said to him: "Our father, she will certainly die because we have left her in great torment, and you are going away for other matters?" And while the emissaries sent by the bride's father-in-law were saying this, tears were flowing from their eyes. The master, who saw hidden things with the help of the Holy Spirit, said: "My children, what is it that makes you weep? As for the bride, she will not die! Only follow me and you will see the great things of the Lord." He went and they followed him, and when he reached the place where that stone idol was, in which the spirit of Satan dwelt, the master said to his disciples: "Go against this unclean one and throw it with force into the depths of the abyss."
ቀላይ ። ወተአዘዙ ፡፤ ለቃሉ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡፤ እስመ ፡ ቀንኡ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ለሕገ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይብል ፡፤ እስመ ፡ ቅንአተ ፡ ቤትከ” ፡ በልዓኒ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጣዖተ ፡ እብን ፡ ቀነቱ ፡ ሐቋሆሙ ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ያንኩርኩርዎ ፡ ወያሥጥምዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልጐተ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ወስእንዎ ፡ አንቀልቅሎቶ ። ወነሥኡ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በርእሶሙ ፡ ወወገሩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘነከዮ ። ወፈጽሕዎ ፡ በመጽሕ ፤ ወኢሰበርዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ እብን ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወዝኩሉ" ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ግብረ ፣፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ”፤ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አርድእቲሁ ፡ ኦአቡነ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ስእናሁ ፡ ወአንተ ፡ ዕትብ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ይረድአከ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፣ እምአፈ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡፤ ተንሥአ ፡፤ ፍጡነ ፡ ወላዕሌሁ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡፤ በከመ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ አማልክቲሆሙ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ። ግብረ ፡ እደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ አፍ” ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢይነቡ ፡ ዓይን” ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢይሬእዩ ፡ እዝን ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢይሰምፁዑ ፡ አንፍ” ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢያፄንዉ ፡ እድ” ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢይገሱ ፡ እግር?" ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወኢየሐውሩ ፡ ወኢይነቡ ፡ በጐራዒቶሙ” ፣ ወአልቦሙ ፡ መንፈስ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉሆሙ ፣ ከማሆሙ ፡ ለይኩኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡እለ ፡ ገብርዎሙ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡እለ፣ ይትአመንዎሙ ።
51 And they obeyed his word and went quickly because they had much zeal for the law of the Lord (2 Cor 11:2; Gal 1:14) according to the Scripture that says: "Zeal for your house consumes me" (Ps 69:9; Jn 2:17). And when they reached the stone idol, they girded their loins and wanted to rotate it and sink it into the depths of the abyss, but they could not move it. They then took a large stone on their heads and threw it upon it, but it did not even scratch it. They struck it with a hammer, but they did not break that stone in which the spirit of Satan dwelt. All this had happened so that the work of God might be manifested in this man (Jn 9:3) of God, Abuna Damyanos. His disciples said to him: "O our father, we have not succeeded, but you make the sign upon it because there is a power that helps you." Having heard this from the mouth of his children, he rose in haste and stood over the stone and opened his mouth, pronouncing the same words of David which say: "The idols of the nations are gold and silver, works of the hand of the son of man; they have mouths and do not speak, they have eyes and do not see, they have ears and do not hear, they have noses and do not smell, they have hands and do not touch, they have feet and do not walk, and with their throats they do not speak, and there is no spirit in their mouths. May those who made them and those who trust in them be like them" (Ps 115:4-8).
ወዘንተ ፣ ብሂሎ ፡ ወረቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትእምርተ" ፡ ሕማም ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወትእምርተ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ዘቅቡኣን ፡ በደመ ፡ ፍርቃን ። ወአንበረ ፡ መልዕልተ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ጣዖተ ፡ እብን ። ወነቅዓ ፡ ወተሰ
52 Having said this, he spat on the cross, which is an instrument of suffering for Satan and a sign of salvation for the believers (1 Cor 1:18) anointed with the blood of redemption (Ex 12:3-27; Heb 9:22). And he placed (the cross) upon that stone idol. his magnificence, they could not answer, so much so that he said to them: "Take (the pieces) one by one and throw them into the depth of the abyss." And as he told them, they took (the idol) and threw it into the depth of the abyss. Thereafter, Abuna Damyanos continued his journey along the way that leads to Dabla Suma where that bride lay sick, and he went surrounded by great grace. And those disciples of his and those messengers of the bride's father-in-law said to one another: "What is this great miracle that we have seen in this chosen father, man of God? Neither we nor our fathers have ever seen or heard of a miracle that has occurred today!"

53 And they continued their journey, marveling at this thing, and they preceded him on the way. During every journey, he had the habit of staying a stone's throw (Lk 22:41) from the people and sang the Psalter of David the prophet with hot tears, looking at the face of heaven and offering honor and glory. And at the end of his daily office, he prostrated himself on his knees and gave glory to his creator. May his blessing preserve [all of us. Amen!] And when those wicked people who worshipped that stone idol learned (of its destruction), they were taken by a heavy sorrow that emanates from their father Satan. However, in the end, they converted and did perfect penance at the hand of this master, after having heard the outpouring of his word, more delicious than drinking the wine that gladdens the heart (Ps 104:15) of many. It is indeed capable of discerning all that is and all that will be. May his blessing guard us. Amen. Abuna Demyanos heals the bride now in a coma.

54 Let us resume the original discourse. When Abuna Damyanos reached the village, the whole village in which that bride was, whom the spirit of the demon had struck, was stirred. Having seen that he had come and entered the village, they hastened to receive him and desired an imprint of his hands, to the point of not allowing him access to the city, so great was the anguish to welcome him. Men and women, adolescents and children, servants and maids flocked: great indeed was the grace that surrounded him, for which strength sustained him, and those people obtained the imprint of his hands. There were then those who gathered the dust of his feet. And there were those who took the

saliva of his mouth, and there were those who came to touch the fringe of his garments: there was indeed a power that went out from his mouth (Lk 8:40ff). They then entered their homes full of joy because they had heard the miracle against the idol, and they were deeply admired and amazed by it. As for the father (and) master, he entered the dwelling where that bride lay and found her unconscious: indeed (the demon) had made her suffer and fall very ill. 55 The master observed her and appealed to his Savior and said: "In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, may all your limbs be calmed!" And he signed her with the sign of the cross. And her whole body was calmed. He opened his mouth (Mt 5:2) and took the book, that is, the Gadl of our Apostolic Fathers; he was indeed a follower of their doctrine and loved them from the depths of his heart. For this reason, they too (the Apostles) love him and protect him in his coming and in his going (Dt 28:6), in all his ways. And having opened the book, he recited it over the water and baptized her, saying: "In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, may the unclean spirit go out from you and may the Holy Spirit who sanctifies the soul dwell in you." And at that moment, that unclean spirit went out like smoke, (she) regained consciousness and put on her clothes and began to cover herself again with her bridal veils as befits a bride, and (Abuna Demyanos) returned her alive (Acts 9:41). And those who had seen her rejoiced and said: "Glory to God who has given such power to the sons of man (Mt 9:8) over unclean spirits and to break stones." May the prayer and blessing of this master be with us. Amen! Amen! The citizens of Hagara Masqal send an embassy to Abuna

### Damyanos
ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ መስቀል ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ጣዖተ" ፡ እብን ፣፤ ዘያመልክዎ ፡ ውሉዶ ፡ አብዳን ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ሰበሮ ፡ በቡራኬሁ ። ወበላዕለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መርዓት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አውፅኦ ፡ ለመንፈሰ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፤ ወውዑ ፡ ወተዋክሑ ፡፤ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ እስመ ፡ ስቁላን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ በአፍቅሮቱ ፡ ወስዉጥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ አስራበ ፡ ትምህርቱ ፡ ዘይጥዕም ፡ እመዓር ፡ ወሦከር ፡፤ ወይትበደር ፡ እምወርቅ ፤ ወእምብሩር ። ወተባሀሉ"' ፡ ፳ምስለ ፡ ካልኡ ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ አንስት ፡ ምስለ ፡ አንስቲያሆሙ ፡ ወወራዙት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወራዙት።
56 And when the inhabitants of Hagara Masqal learned of the miracle that Abuna Demyanos had performed on the stone idol that the children of fools used to worship and that he had shattered it with his blessing, and how he had expelled the spirit of Satan from that bride, they cried out and were stirred and rejoiced greatly. They were carried away by his love, and their hearts were flooded by the cascades of his teaching, more pleasant than the honeycomb and honey, preferable to gold and silver (Ps 19:10). And they spoke to one another, those who had wives with their wives, the young with the young, and (with) the shepherds. And everyone spoke with his neighbor. And they took counsel

with one another, saying: "This world, what will it profit us? It must pass for us after a short time: its joys and its beauties will also pass. As our father Clement says, who, admonishing us with his spiritual word suggested to him by our Father Peter, says: 'Kings have passed away and dignitaries have fallen into oblivion and the rich have died. And the women adorned with gold and silver jewels and precious pearls, where are they? Have they not all passed away?' Indeed, the life of this world is fleeting (1 Cor 7:31). Its joy is little and its beauty is perishing (Jas 1:11). Wealth and glory cannot be recovered, love for children and relatives will be forgotten because living in this world is transitory."
ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም። ወበዝ'' ፡ ነገር ፡ ኃብሩ ፡ ወሠምሩ ፡ ወተሰናአዉ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ወኢያ ስተዳኅሩ ፡ እንተ ፡ መከሩ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ይጋጐኡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ዝአቡነ ፡ ማእዜ ፤ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤነ ። ወለአኩ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አንተ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ለምንትአ ፡ ትገብርአ ፡ ከመዝአ ፡ ዘትነብርአ ፡ ህየአ ፡ ወትጐነዲአ ፡ ኃዲገአ ፡ መርዔትከአ” ፡ ዘአ ስተብዛኅከአ ፡ በጸሎትከአ ፡ ወቦኑአ ፡ ዘይተክልአ ፡ ወይነአ” ፡ ወየኀድግአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይስቂዮአ ፡ ወቦኑአ ፡ ዘይዘርእአ ፡ ገራህተአ” ፡ ወኢይከልእአ ፡ አስዋከአ ፡ ወአሜከለአ ።
57 They agreed on (all) this and were pleased with it; they therefore agreed to put into practice immediately, without procrastinating, what they had counseled. Indeed, they were in a hurry and asked themselves: "When will this father of ours come to us?" They therefore sent an embassy to him and said to him: "You, our father, why do you behave in this way? You are (living) here, yet you delay, abandoning your flock that you have multiplied with your prayer? Is there perhaps someone who plants a vine and leaves it without watering it, or is there perhaps someone who sows a field and does not
ለወይንኒአ ፡ ለእመአ ፡ ኢሰቀይዎአ ፡ አልቦአ ፡ ዘይበቀ-ዕአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘይየብ ስአ ፤ ወለዘርእኒአ ፡ ለእመአ ፤ ኢመሐዉአ ፡ እምኔሁአ ፡ አስዋኪሁአ ፡ አልቦአ ፡ ዘይበቀ-ዕአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘየሐንቆአ ፡ ሦክአ ፡ ወአሜከላአ ፡፤ ወኢያበውሕአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፍረይ ፡ ፍሬአ ። ወሰብእኒአ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ሰምዓአ ፡ ቃለ ፤ ትምህርትአ ፡ ወገብአአ ፡ መንገለአ ፡ አሚነአ ። ወለእመአ ፡ ኢተወግዐ ፡ አ"፤ ፡ በሐሊበ ፡ ትምህርትአ ፡ ይገብእአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ፡ ክህደቱአ ፡ ወይከውንአ ፡ ከመአ ፡ ዘኢተምህረአ ። 'ወውእቱኒአ ፡ መምህርአ ፡ አልቦአ ፡ ምዝጋናአ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይከውንአ ፡ ጸማሁአ ፡ በከአ” ። ወውእቱ ኒአ” ፡ ባዕለ ፡ ዐፀደ ፡ ወይንአ ፡ የኃጐልአ ፡ እምነአ ፡ ጸማሁአ ፡ ወለኩሎሙአ ፡ ከንቶአ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጸማሆሙአ” ። ወይእዜኒአ ፡ አፍጥንአ ፡ ኀቤነአ ፡ እስመአ ፡ ትሴፈወከአ" ፡ ኩላአ ፡ ሀገርአ ፡ ከመአ ፡ ትሴፎአ ፡ ጽምእትአ ፡ ምድርአ ፡ ዝናመአ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ” ፡ መልእክተአ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ አብ ፡ ክቡር ፡ በመንፈሱ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አአኩተከ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘተወከፍከ ፡ ለአሚኖትየ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ እንተ ፡ይእቲ ፡ አረሞ ። ወበዊኦ ፡ሀገረ ፡ ተቀበልዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ገባሬ ፡ ተአምር።
remove the thorns and thistles? 58 "If the vine is not watered, it will be of no use except to dry up. Likewise the plants: if one does not tear out their thorns, they will be of no use. The thorns and thistles will choke them and they will not be able to bear fruit. And also man, if once he has heard the word of teaching (of the Gospel) and embraced the faith, if he is not nourished with the milk of teaching (1 Cor 3:2; Heb 5:12-13; 1 Pt 2:2), he will return to his original apostasy and will become like one who has not studied. Therefore, that master will have no reward because his labors will become vain. And that master of the vineyard will remain without the fruits of his labor, and the labor of all will be in vain. Therefore, come to us quickly, because the whole country hopes in you as the dry land hopes for rain." Having heard this message, the venerable Father rejoiced in his spirit and said: "I thank you, O my Lord Jesus Christ (Lk 10:21), who have accepted my faith."


[p. 184] {#po.50.p184}

Triumphal entry into Hagara Masqal. Many citizens decide to become monks. 59 And having said this, he rose and went to Hagara Masqal, that is, Aramo. Having entered the village, they welcomed him, saying: "That wonderworker has arrived. That one has arrived
